#703 [D4 Cost] [ANS D]
Q: A company will migrate 10 PB of data to Amazon S3 in 6 weeks. The current data center has a 500 Mbps uplink to the internet. Other on-premises applications share the uplink. The company can use 80% of the internet bandwidth for this one-time migration task. Which solution will meet these requirements?
CORRECT: D: Order multiple AWS Snowball devices. Copy the data to the devices. Send the devices to AWS to copy the data to Amazon S3.
WHY: 10 PB cannot move over a shared 500 Mbps link within 6 weeks, so ordering multiple AWS Snowball devices and shipping them to AWS is the only solution that meets the deadline.

Which solution will meet these requirements?

WHY: An AWS Storage Gateway volume gateway with cached volumes keeps frequently used data cached locally for low latency while storing the full dataset in Amazon S3, reducing on-premises servers with minimal changes.

#711 [D1 Secure] [ANS D]

CORRECT: C: Create a read replica from the source DB instance. Serve read traffic from the read replica.
WHY: After you create a read replica from a source DB instance, the source becomes the primary DB instance. When you make updates to the primary DB instance, Amazon RDS copies them asynchronously to the read replica.

#741 [D3 Performance] [ANS A]

CORRECT: A: Attach a Network Load Balancer to the Auto Scaling group.
WHY: A Network Load Balancer operates at Layer 4 and supports UDP listeners, so it can front the Auto Scaling group serving UDP gaming traffic and scale with demand. An ALB (Layer 7) does not support UDP.

The daily peak I/O transactions per second do not exceed 15,000 IOPS. The company wants to migrate the database to Amazon RDS for PostgreSQL and provision disk IOPS performance independent of disk storage capacity. Which solution will meet these requirements MOST cost-effectively?
CORRECT: C: Configure the General Purpose SSD (gp3) EBS volume storage type and provision 15,000 IOPS.
WHY: A General Purpose SSD (gp3) volume lets IOPS be provisioned independently of storage capacity (up to 16,000 IOPS) and is cheaper than Provisioned IOPS (io1) for the 15,000-IOPS peak.

#749 [D2 Resilient] [ANS A]

The data analysis team uses the same production database to run reports for analytical processing. The company wants to reduce operational overhead by moving to managed services wherever possible. Which solution will meet these requirements with the LEAST operational overhead?
CORRECT: A: Migrate to Amazon RDS for Microsoft SOL Server. Use read replicas for reporting purposes
WHY: Amazon RDS for Microsoft SQL Server is a managed service that supports the Enterprise edition; read replicas offload the analytical reporting from the transactional workload — least operational overhead.

#751 [D4 Cost] [ANS B]

CORRECT: B: Configure a transient cluster that runs the primary node and core nodes on On-Demand Instances and the task nodes on Spot Instances.
WHY: A transient cluster provides cost savings because it runs only during the computation time, and it provides scalability and flexibility in a cloud environment.

WHY: AWS Shield Advanced provides managed DDoS protection and access to the AWS DDoS Response Team, with minimal configuration changes and an audit trail of attack sources.

#803 [D3 Performance] [ANS C]
Q: An online video game company must maintain ultra-low latency for its game servers. The game servers run on Amazon EC2 instances. The company needs a solution that can handle millions of UDP internet traffic requests each second. Which solution will meet these requirements MOST cost-effectively?

WHY: A Network Load Balancer handles millions of UDP requests per second at Layer 4 with ultra-low latency and is the most cost-effective option — an ALB cannot serve UDP.

#824 [D4 Cost] [ANS B]

CORRECT: B: Enable an S3 Lifecycle policy that deletes incomplete multipart uploads.
WHY: Failed or abandoned multipart uploads leave incomplete parts that still incur storage charges; an S3 Lifecycle policy that deletes incomplete multipart uploads removes this hidden, growing cost.

#830 [D2 Resilient] [ANS C]

CORRECT: C: Request strongly consistent reads for the table.
WHY: Requests not returning the latest data indicate eventually consistent reads; requesting strongly consistent reads on the DynamoDB table ensures each read returns the most recent committed write.

CORRECT: A: Create an Amazon Route 53 public hosted zone for the domain name. Import the zone file containing the domain records hosted by the previous provider.
WHY: Creating an Amazon Route 53 public hosted zone and importing the existing zone file rapidly migrates DNS hosting to the resilient, managed Route 53 service.

#841 [D1 Secure] [ANS A]
Q: A company is building an application on AWS that connects to an Amazon RDS database. The company wants to manage the application configuration and to securely store and retrieve credentials for the database and other services. Which solution will meet these requirements with the LEAST administrative overhead?
CORRECT: A: Use AWS AppConfig to store and manage the application configuration. Use AWS Secrets Manager to store and retrieve the credentials.
WHY: AWS AppConfig manages the application configuration and AWS Secrets Manager securely stores, retrieves, and rotates the database and service credentials — both managed services, least administrative overhead.

#842 [D1 Secure] [ANS D]
Q: To meet security requirements, a company needs to encrypt all of its application data in transit while communicating with an Amazon RDS MySQL DB instance. A recent security audit revealed that encryption at rest is enabled using AWS Key Management Service (AWS KMS), but data in transit is not enabled. What should a solutions architect do to satisfy the security requirements?
CORRECT: D: Download AWS-provided root certificates. Provide the certificates in all connections to the RDS instance.
WHY: Downloading the AWS-provided RDS root certificates and using them in all client connections enables TLS/SSL encryption of data in transit to the RDS MySQL instance.

#843 [D2 Resilient] [ANS C]
Q: A company is designing a new web service that will run on Amazon EC2 instances behind an Elastic Load Balancing (ELB) load balancer. However, many of the web service clients can only reach IP addresses authorized on their firewalls. What should a solutions architect recommend to meet the clients' needs?
CORRECT: C: An A record in an Amazon Route 53 hosted zone pointing to an Elastic IP address.
WHY: Clients can only whitelist fixed IP addresses, so an Amazon Route 53 A record pointing to a static Elastic IP address gives them a stable IP to authorize on their firewalls.

#844 [D1 Secure] [ANS B]
Q: A company has established a new AWS account. The account is newly provisioned and no changes have been made to the default settings. The company is concerned about the security of the AWS account root user. What should be done to secure the root user?
CORRECT: B: Create IAM users for daily administrative tasks. Enable multi-factor authentication on the root user.
WHY: Securing a new account’s root user means creating IAM users for daily administrative work and enabling multi-factor authentication on the root user — the root user should not be used routinely or have access keys.

Which combination of network solutions will meet these requirements? (Choose two.)

WHY: Enhanced networking gives each EC2 instance higher packet-per-second performance and lower latency, and a cluster placement group packs the instances close together — together they provide the lowest node-to-node latency

#887 [D4 Cost] [ANS C]

CORRECT: C: Create an AWS Glue crawler to store and retrieve table metadata from the S3 bucket. Use Amazon Athena to run SQL statements directly on the data in th
WHY: An AWS Glue crawler catalogs the Parquet data and Amazon Athena runs SQL directly on it in S3, with pay-per-query pricing — the most cost-effective option for occasional analysis (no cluster to run).

#888 [D1 Secure] [ANS D]
Q: A company needs a solution to prevent AWS CloudFormation stacks from deploying AWS Identity and Access Management (IAM) resources that include an inline policy or "*" in the statement. The solution must also prohibit deployment of Amazon EC2 instances with public IP addresses. The company has AWS Control Tower enabled in its organization in AWS Organizations. Which solution will meet these requirements?
CORRECT: D: Use a service control policy (SCP) to block actions for the EC2 instances and IAM resources if the actions lead to noncompliance.
WHY: A service control policy blocks the noncompliant actions — creating IAM resources with inline policies or wildcards and launching EC2 instances with public IPs — preventively across the organization.

#889 [D2 Resilient] [ANS BE]

The web application has not been able to meet the demand of the increased web traffic. CORRECT: B: Configure Amazon EC2 Auto Scaling with multiple Availability Zones in private subnets. | E: Configure an Application Load Balancer in a public subnet to distribute web traffic.
WHY: Configuring EC2 Auto Scaling across multiple Availability Zones in private subnets provides scalability and high availability, and an internet-facing Application Load Balancer in a public subnet distributes the web traff

#890 [D1 Secure] [ANS D]
Q: A company has AWS Lambda functions that use environment variables. The company does not want its developers to see environment variables in plaintext. Which solution will meet these requirements?
CORRECT: D: Create an AWS Key Management Service (AWS KMS) key. Enable encryption helpers on the Lambda functions to use the KMS key to store and encrypt the envi
WHY: Creating an AWS KMS key and enabling Lambda encryption helpers encrypts the environment variables so developers cannot see them in plaintext in the console.

#891 [D1 Secure] [ANS A]
Q: An analytics company uses Amazon VPC to run its multi-tier services. The company wants to use RESTful APIs to offer a web analytics service to millions of users. Users must be verified by using an authentication service to access the APIs. Which solution will meet these requirements with the MOST operational efficiency?
CORRECT: A: Configure an Amazon Cognito user pool for user authentication. Implement Amazon API Gateway REST APIs with a Cognito authorizer.
WHY: An Amazon Cognito user pool authenticates the millions of users, and Amazon API Gateway REST APIs with a Cognito authorizer verify each request — the most operationally efficient managed solution.

#892 [D1 Secure] [ANS C]

The company needs a solution that prevents the accidental deletion of KMS keys. The solution must use Amazon Simple Notification Service (Amazon SNS) to send an email notification to administrators when a user attempts to de Which solution will meet these requirements with the LEAST operational overhead?
CORRECT: C: Create an Amazon EventBridge rule that reacts when the KMS DeleteKey operation is performed. Configure the rule to initiate an AWS Systems Manager Aut
WHY: An EventBridge rule that reacts to the KMS DeleteKey operation can trigger an automated response and an SNS email notification to administrators with the least operational overhead — KMS already enforces a key-deletion w

#893 [D4 Cost] [ANS B]
Q: A company wants to analyze and generate reports to track the usage of its mobile app. The app is popular and has a global user base. The company uses a custom report building program to analyze application usage. The program generates multiple reports during the last week of each month. The program takes less than 10 minutes to produce each report. The company rarely uses the p Which solution will meet these requirements MOST cost-effectively?
CORRECT: B: Run the program in AWS Lambda. Create an Amazon EventBridge rule to run a Lambda function when reports are requested.
WHY: Because the report program runs briefly (under 10 minutes) and only during the last week of each month, running it in AWS Lambda triggered by EventBridge costs nothing when idle — the most cost-effective option.

#894 [D3 Performance] [ANS BD]
Q: A company is designing a tightly coupled high performance computing (HPC) environment in the AWS Cloud. The company needs to include features that will optimize the HPC environment for networking and storage. Which combination of solutions will meet these requirements? (Choose two.) Which combination of solutions will meet these requirements? (Choose two.)
CORRECT: B: Create an Amazon FSx for Lustre file system. Configure the file system with scratch storage. | D: Launch Amazon EC2 instances. Attach an Elastic Fabric Adapter (EFA) to the instances.
WHY: An Amazon FSx for Lustre scratch file system provides the high-performance storage, and an Elastic Fabric Adapter on the EC2 instances provides the low-latency, high-throughput networking for the tightly coupled HPC work

#895 [D1 Secure] [ANS B]
Q: A company needs a solution to prevent photos with unwanted content from being uploaded to the company's web application. The solution must not involve training a machine learning (ML) model. Which solution will meet these requirements?
CORRECT: B: Create an AWS Lambda function that uses Amazon Rekognition to detect unwanted content. Create a Lambda function URL that the web application invokes w
WHY: An AWS Lambda function that calls Amazon Rekognition detects unwanted image content using a pre-built service, so no machine learning model needs to be trained; a Lambda function URL lets the web application invoke it.

#896 [D1 Secure] [ANS B]
Q: A company uses AWS to run its ecommerce platform. The platform is critical to the company's operations and has a high volume of traffic and transactions. The company configures a multi- factor authentication (MFA) device to secure its AWS account root user credentials. The company wants to ensure that it will not lose access to the root user account if the MFA device is lost. Which solution will meet these requirements?
CORRECT: B: Add multiple MFA devices for the root user account to handle the disaster scenario.
WHY: Adding multiple MFA devices to the root user account means the company can still sign in with another registered device if one MFA device is lost, avoiding loss of root access.

#897 [D2 Resilient] [ANS B]
Q: A social media company is creating a rewards program website for its users. The company gives users points when users create and upload videos to the website. Users redeem their points for gifts or discounts from the company's affiliated partners. A unique ID identifies users. The partners refer to this ID to verify user eligibility for rewards The partners want to receive noti Which solution will meet these requirements with the LEAST implementation effort?
CORRECT: B: Create an Amazon Simple Notification Service (Amazon SNS) topic. Choose an endpoint protocol. Subscribe the partners to the topic. Publish user IDs to
WHY: An Amazon SNS topic with an HTTP/HTTPS endpoint protocol lets hundreds of partners simply subscribe and receive user-ID notifications when points are awarded — the least implementation effort.
